===Interpretation:

AI explainener: 
1. Birth name (maiden name)
Elisabeth Barth
geb. = geboren, “born as.”
This is always the earliest name in the sequence.
💍 2. First marriage
She married a man with the surname Schenk.
After that marriage, her legal/married name became:
Elisabeth Schenk
⚰️ 3. Widowhood
verw. Schenk = verwitwet Schenk, “widowed (from) Schenk.”
This tells you her first husband (Mr. Schenk) died.
💍 4. Second marriage
She later married a man named Götz.
Her final married name at death was:
Elisabeth Götz
